@SpecialTransformations.Section3.rvTerm_local : ∀ {M : Type} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {A : Set M} {Γ : Type}
  [inst_1 : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ] [inst_2 : Valued SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType Γ]
  (p : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Parameters A) (E : Subfield SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
  (∀ (i : Fin p.length) (h : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF (p.values i)), ⟨p.values i, h⟩ ∈ E) →
    (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
        SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ Valued.v x ≤ Valued.v y) →
      ∀ (a : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
        Transcendental (↥E) a →
          ∀ (t : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Term (Fin p.length ⊕ Fin 1)),
            SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.TmSort p.sorts (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf)
                  t =
                some SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv →
              {x |
                  FirstOrder.Language.Term.realize (Sum.elim p.values ![↑x]) t =

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.corollary_3_4 : ∀ {M : Type} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] (S : Set M)
  (F : SpecialTransformations.Section3.VFFieldBase S) {n : ℕ}
  (a : Fin n → SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType) (B : Set M)
  (hB : ∀ b ∈ B, SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV b),
  let A := S ∪ Set.range fun i => ↑(a i);
  let E := SpecialTransformations.Section3.vfParameterField A;
  let D := SpecialTransformations.Section3.vfDefinableClosureField (A ∪ B);
  let C := SpecialTransformations.Section3.vfAlgebraicClosureField (A ∪ B);
  have hSE := ⋯;
  have hED := ⋯;
  have hDC := ⋯;
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.relativeTrdeg F.field E hSE =
      SpecialTransformations.Section3.relativeTrdeg F.field D ⋯ ∧
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.relativeTrdeg F.field E hSE =
      SpecialTransformations.Section3.relativeTrdeg F.field C ⋯
'SpecialTransformations.Section3.corollary_3_4' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  [inst_1 : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ] [IsAlgClosed K] (v : Valuation K Γ),
  (∃ x, x ≠ 0 ∧ v x < 1) →
    ∀ {ι : Type} (s : Finset ι) (D : ι → Set K),
      (∀ i ∈ s, SpecialTransformations.Section3.ClosedBallRoots.IsClosedBall v (D i)) →
        (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, i ≠ j → Disjoint (D i) (D j)) →

                                      ys)))))

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.corollary_3_12 : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [hM :
    @FirstOrder.Language.Theory.Model SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV M inst
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {m : Nat} (S B : Set M)
  (f :
    @Set.Elem (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
        (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.RVFibreConstancy.domain M inst B) →
      Fin m → M),
  (∀
      (a :
        @Set.Elem (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
          (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.RVFibreConstancy.domain M inst B))
      (i : Fin m), @SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV M inst (f a i)) →
    @SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver M inst S
        (@HAdd.hAdd Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd Nat instAddNat) m (@OfNat.ofNat Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
        (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.MixedPolynomialSyntax.contextSort m)
        (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.RVFibreConstancy.graph M inst m B f) →
      @Set.Finite M
        (@Set.ofPred M fun t =>
          And (@Membership.mem M (Set M) (@Set.instMembership M) B t)
            (∃ a b,
              And
                (@Eq M
                  (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRv M inst
                    (@Subtype.val M (fun x => @SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF M inst x)
                      (@Subtype.val (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                        (fun x =>
                          @Membership.mem (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                            (Set (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst))
                            (@Set.instMembership (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst))
                            (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.RVFibreConstancy.domain M inst B) x)
                        a)))
                  t)
                (And
                  (@Eq M
                    (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRv M inst
                      (@Subtype.val M (fun x => @SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF M inst x)
                        (@Subtype.val (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                          (fun x =>
                            @Membership.mem (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                              (Set (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst))
                              (@Set.instMembership (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst))
                              (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.RVFibreConstancy.domain M inst B) x)
                          b)))
                    t)
                  (@Ne (Fin m → M) (f a) (f b)))))

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.valueEquiv : {M : Type} →
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM :
        @FirstOrder.Language.Theory.Model SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV M inst
          SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →
      {Γ : Type} →
        [inst_1 : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ] →
          (v :
              @Valuation (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst) Γ
                (@LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero.toLinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero Γ inst_1)
                (@DivisionRing.toRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                  (@Field.toDivisionRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                    (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.instFieldVFType M inst hM)))) →
            (∀ (x y : @SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst),
                Iff (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV M inst x y)
                  (@LE.le Γ
                    (@Preorder.toLE Γ
                      (@PartialOrder.toPreorder Γ
                        (@SemilatticeInf.toPartialOrder Γ
                          (@Lattice.toSemilatticeInf Γ
                            (@DistribLattice.toLattice Γ
                              (@instDistribLatticeOfLinearOrder Γ
                                (@LinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero.toLinearOrder Γ
                                  (@LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero.toLinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero Γ inst_1))))))))
                    (@DFunLike.coe
                      (@Valuation (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst) Γ
                        (@LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero.toLinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero Γ inst_1)
                        (@DivisionRing.toRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                          (@Field.toDivisionRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                            (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.instFieldVFType M inst hM))))
                      (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst) (fun x => Γ)
                      (@Valuation.instFunLike (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst) Γ
                        (@DivisionRing.toRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                          (@Field.toDivisionRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)
                            (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.instFieldVFType M inst hM)))
                        (@LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero.toLinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero Γ inst_1))
                      v x)
                    (@DFunLike.coe
                      (@Valuation (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst) Γ
                        (@LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero.toLinearOrderedCommMonoidWithZero Γ inst_1)
                        (@DivisionRing.toRing (@SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst)

              Equiv (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.GammaTop M inst hM)

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.valueEquiv_rv_mul : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {Γ : Type} [inst_1 : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ]
  (v : Valuation SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType Γ)
  (hv :
    ∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ v x ≤ v y)
  (t u : CanonicalRV.RVSort M),
  (SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.valueEquiv v hv)
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv ⟨SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVMul ↑t ↑u, ⋯⟩) =
    (SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.valueEquiv v hv) (SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv t) *
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.valueEquiv v hv) (SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv u)
'SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.valueEquiv_rv_mul' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM :
        @FirstOrder.Language.Theory.Model SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV M inst
          SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →

          (v :

            (∀ (x y : @SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType M inst),

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.ClosedQuotientChoice.choose_profile : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {Γ : Type} [inst : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ]
  (v : Valuation SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType Γ),
  (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ v x ≤ v y) →
    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ (S : Set M) {Q : Type} (q : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType → Q),
        Function.Surjective q →
          ∀ (E : Set (Fin 2 → M)),
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver S (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf) E →
              (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType), ![↑x, ↑y] ∈ E ↔ q x = q y) →
                (∀ (d : Q), SpecialTransformations.Section3.ClosedBallRoots.IsClosedBall v {x | q x = d}) →
                  ∀ {m : ℕ} (f : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType → Set (Fin m → M)),
                    (∀ (x : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
                        ∀ ys ∈ f x, ∀ (i : Fin m), SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (ys i)) →
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver S
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.MixedPolynomialSyntax.contextSort
                          (SpecialTransformations.Section3.BallFibreConstancy.graph f) →
                        ∃ G,
                          (∀ (d : Q), ∀ ys ∈ G d, ∀ (i : Fin m), SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (ys i)) ∧
                            SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver S
                                SpecialTransformations.Section3.MixedPolynomialSyntax.contextSort
                                (SpecialTransformations.Section3.BallFibreConstancy.graph fun x => G (q x)) ∧
                              ∀ (d : Q), ∃ x, q x = d ∧ f x = G d

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation.full_preimage : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [hM :
    @FirstOrder.Language.Theory.Model SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV M sM
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {k n : Nat} {sn : Fin n → SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag}
  (R : @SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation M sM hM S k n sn)
  (t : Fin k → @SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Code M sM) (b : Fin n → M),
  Iff
    (@Membership.mem (Fin n → M) (Set (Fin n → M)) (@Set.instMembership (Fin n → M))
      (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation.fibre M sM hM S k n sn R
        (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.values M sM hM k t))
      b)
    (@Membership.mem (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)) (Set (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)))
      (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)))
      (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation.carrier M sM hM S k n sn R)
      (@Prod.mk (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M) (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.rawTuple M sM k t) b))
'SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation.full_preimage' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

    ∀ (S B : Set M),
      @LE.le (Set M) (@Set.instLE M) S B →
        ∀ (A : Set (Fin n → M)) (b : M),
          @LT.lt Cardinal.{0}
              (@Preorder.toLT Cardinal.{0} (@PartialOrder.toPreorder Cardinal.{0} Cardinal.partialOrder))
              (Cardinal.mk (@Set.Elem M (@insert M (Set M) (@Set.instInsert M) b B))) κ →
            @Set.Definable M B SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV inst (Fin n) A →

                  @Membership.mem (Fin n → M) (Set (Fin n → M)) (@Set.instMembership (Fin n → M)) A a →
                    @SpecialTransformations.Section3.RawClosure.AlgebraicOver M inst
                      (@Union.union (Set M) (@Set.instUnion M) S (@Set.range M (Fin n) a)) b) →
                ∃ R,
                  ∀ (a : Fin n → M),
                    @Membership.mem (Fin n → M) (Set (Fin n → M)) (@Set.instMembership (Fin n → M)) A a →
                      @Membership.mem (Prod (Fin n → M) M) (Set (Prod (Fin n → M) M))
                        (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin n → M) M))
                        (@Subtype.val (Set (Prod (Fin n → M) M))
                          (fun R =>
                            And (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.RawClosure.Tuple.RelationDefinable M inst n S R)
                              (∀ (a : Fin n → M),
                                @Set.Finite M
                                  (@Set.ofPred M fun x =>
                                    @Membership.mem (Prod (Fin n → M) M) (Set (Prod (Fin n → M) M))
                                      (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin n → M) M)) R (@Prod.mk (Fin n → M) M a x))))
                          R)

@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation.mk : {M : Type} →
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM :
        @FirstOrder.Language.Theory.Model SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV M sM
          SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →
      {S : Set M} →
        {k n : Nat} →
          {sn : Fin n → SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag} →
            (carrier : Set (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M))) →
              @Set.Definable M S SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV (@CanonicalStructure.normalize M sM)
                  (Sum (Fin k) (Fin n))
                  (@Set.ofPred (Sum (Fin k) (Fin n) → M) fun z =>
                    @Membership.mem (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)) (Set (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)))
                      (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M))) carrier
                      (@Prod.mk (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)
                        (@Function.comp (Fin k) (Sum (Fin k) (Fin n)) M z (@Sum.inl (Fin k) (Fin n)))
                        (@Function.comp (Fin n) (Sum (Fin k) (Fin n)) M z (@Sum.inr (Fin k) (Fin n))))) →
                (∀ (p : Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)),
                    @Membership.mem (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)) (Set (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)))
                        (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M))) carrier p →
                      And
                        (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Proper M sM k
                          (@Prod.fst (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M) p))
                        (∀ (i : Fin n),
                          @SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.sortEval M sM (sn i)
                            (@Prod.snd (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M) p i))) →
                  (∀ (t u : Fin k → @SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Code M sM),
                      @Eq (Fin k → @SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma M sM hM)
                          (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.values M sM hM k t)
                          (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.values M sM hM k u) →
                        ∀ (b : Fin n → M),
                          Iff
                            (@Membership.mem (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)) (Set (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)))
                              (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M))) carrier
                              (@Prod.mk (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)
                                (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.rawTuple M sM k t) b))
                            (@Membership.mem (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)) (Set (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)))
                              (@Set.instMembership (Prod (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M))) carrier
                              (@Prod.mk (Fin k → M) (Fin n → M)
                                (@SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.rawTuple M sM k u) b))) →
                    @SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Relation M sM hM S k n sn
